Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The recognition operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 554. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 80 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to recognition structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 554/80 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The recognition variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 496 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 177 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 496 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the recognition structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 177. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
